definition{
  name: boundary,
  parameters: [],
  result: String,
  body: "Production bounded frame syntax parser. Integrity.Profile.select is the format/machine authority: preserve format2 CHAIN-v1 and explicit format3 STREAM-MAX-v1. No checksum identity is redefined. Each header byte/ULEB digit/span/ack/pending/failed prefix is prepaid, and no payload bytes are copied. Exactly one EOF after the 32-byte integrity region permits endArchive; it is syntax completion, not hash/admission/execution acceptance. Program body spans stop exactly at bodyLength; the controller gives ProgramParse its own EOF there, never integrity bytes. Pending spans remain under the charged wire input owner until consumed or a retained image ticket is transferred. Every objectLength must be acknowledged with its actual charged indexed metadata row before parsing continues; the LengthStore remains owner of that table and the parser retains only a borrowed Table control. Empty lengths are not omitted. State contains only fixed-width controls, handles and one pending event, no recursive AST/list/bytes/rope/cache. Session moves the same actual Arena and Usage; immutable predecessors must not be retained by the controller. Cancellation/failure retain roots; cleanup drains achieved releases. Operational U64 exhaustion refuses the operation without narrowing the mathematical finite wire format. Physical allocation/copy elision and general refinement are separate obligations.",
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext]
}
\end{semanticmodule}
\end{lexlean}
